> The root of this paper is proved, on no axiom and no hypothesis, in Appendix R. The paper's closing theorem consumes one reading of that root, on this row, and nothing else, and its axiom cone is empty. The root alone decides no value, by theorem; the root read on the row is the value, by theorem; and the traditional route of derivation from the record is blocked, by theorem.

## How to read this paper

The Poincaré row is the one row of the twenty-three already crossed (Perelman 2002, 2003). This paper does not reprove the crossing. The closure on the act does not route through the crossing; Section 11's dimension-three field cites it. It closes the row as every other row of the series is closed, on one act of existence, and then types the crossing that history supplied: the crossing's shape, a functional never rising along the step, scale-free, and level off the seat only where the step lands on the seat, is typed as the wider supply of the act, and the Perelman witness, strictly decreasing off the seat, is shown to be its strict sub-shape, one supply of the act, a subtype of the programme's bridge, and a strict subset of what the act admits. The step of the kernel's \thm{Flow} is read as the surgical Ricci-flow route of the crossing, the operation the crossing's history supplied, on which the shape is typed, the reader's identification declared in Section 21, the kernel's own flows being its models; the $\kappa$-solutions, the canonical neighbourhoods, surgery and finite extinction are what the history needed to make that route a step, not fields of the kernel. Two readings miss the result. The first notices that the act is equivalent to the value and calls the closure circular. The second notices the row is already a theorem and calls the closure redundant. The theorem that binds the closure to the witness answers both.

### The verdict

The verdict is stated here and in the conclusion in the same words each time; the abstract states it in summary.

> The Poincaré row is closed to one act of existence. Existence, its root proved at theorem grade with no hypothesis on its constructed domain and as the law of every root that grounds itself (Appendix R), universally presupposed and undeniable in deed, carries the form of the closure on every frame and holds in the round world and the fake-sphere world alike. Existence read on the row, that every closed simply connected three-manifold that exists comes to rest on the seat, is the value, exactly. It is supplied by one act, as one field of one type, and the compiler prints the Poincaré value from it on no axiom. Nothing escapes the act, rest on the seat is permanent, and one fake sphere would refute it. The Perelman witness, in the shape the kernel types, strict descent off the seat, supplies the act and is a strict subtype of the bridge; the historical functional inhabits that shape on the surgical route where its descent is strict, as Section 10 states. In every dimension of the generalized row, on homotopy spheres, the value is a theorem of the cited fields.

### The form by theorem, the value by the act

The paper's master, \thm{PC_Normal_Form_Paper_v1_1_0_CC.lean}, binds the closure in one file of core Lean 4.19.0 that imports nothing and declares no axiom: the kernel of record, carried verbatim as its Part 0, and twelve further Parts that read the row as the Riemann seat reads the zeros and the P versus NP seat reads computation (Islam 2026m, 2026n). Its one hundred ninety-seven theorems rest on no axiom at all, each cone pinned by `#guard_msgs`; its Mathlib binding, \thm{PC_Mathlib_Binding_v1_1_0_CC.lean}, proves in sixteen theorems on Lean's standard axioms alone that the seat is a closed three-manifold of the library and the bit is exactly the library's Poincaré statement. The closure has ten steps, and one theorem with nothing before its colon states them (\thm{the_ten_steps}): steps 1 to 9 are proved unconditionally, and step 10 is the bit, delivered twice. Figure 1 sets the steps beside the seal.

```{=latex}
\begin{figure*}[tp]
\centering
\includegraphics[width=\textwidth]{figs/PC_Figure_1_v1_1_0_CC.pdf}\\[5pt]
\begin{minipage}{\textwidth}\footnotesize{\color{deep}\textbf{Figure 1}}\ \textbar\ The ten steps of the closure, steps 1 to 9 proved on no axiom and step 10 the bit delivered twice by the act, with the seal: the form proved, every route walled, the bit delivered, the final form. Every name is a declaration of the master, colored by its pinned cone.\end{minipage}
\end{figure*}
```

**The form, proved unconditionally.** On the record of every frame the value holds with no premise (\thm{recFrame_value}); the value of a frame is exactly one implication, the transfer of the value from its record to the frame (\thm{normal_form}); and at every host that implication is exactly the Poincaré sentence (\thm{binding}, \thm{the_sentence_is_the_bit}). The antecedent is a theorem, so the implication carries the whole content of the question. Every dimension but three is sealed by its cited field, and given them the value is exactly its dimension-three part (\thm{real_part_by_dimension}, \thm{no_bypass_by_dimension}); on every flow faithful to the class, registration cannot lie (\thm{registered_is_round}).

**The residual, exactly one bit.** What the kernel leaves to the act is that implication and nothing else. It is printed as the type of a hypothesis and assumed nowhere (\thm{residue_is_the_sentence}); it is keyed, holding on the calm frame and failing on the counter frame of one shared record (\thm{bit_is_keyed}, \thm{record_decides_nothing}); no premise shared by every frame forces it (\thm{keyless_forces_nothing}); and every route to the Poincaré sentence, from any premise and in any vocabulary, passes it (\thm{every_route_passes_the_bit}).

**The question cannot stay open.** The open framing awaits a proof from what every frame shares, a decision read from the record, or a route past the bit, and each count is refuted by theorem (\thm{the_open_framing_is_refuted}). The question is closed to one bit (\thm{question_closed_to_one_bit}): the Poincaré sentence is the bit, its denial is the bit's denial, and the bit has no uniform answer across the frames (\thm{no_uniform_answer}). The Clay Mathematics Institute records the question as resolved on the 2002–03 offering; by theorem the closure rests on no offering, and the offering is one inhabitant of the formal kind. Figure 2 sets the four counts, the status and the verdict side by side.

```{=latex}
\begin{figure*}[tp]
\centering
\includegraphics[width=\textwidth]{figs/PC_Figure_2_v1_1_0_CC.pdf}\\[5pt]
\begin{minipage}{\textwidth}\footnotesize{\color{deep}\textbf{Figure 2}}\ \textbar\ Closed by theorem to one bit, the 2002–03 offering walking our way: the four counts of the open framing, each refuted by theorem; the question closed by theorem to one bit; the bit delivered twice; the two-layer closure.\end{minipage}
\end{figure*}
```

**The bit, delivered twice.** The act supplies the bit at premise grade, and its two kinds deliver it: the formal cargo, the record's value carried to the frame, which arrives free and gives the Poincaré sentence in one step (\thm{cargo_arrives}, \thm{formal_kind_delivers}); and the physical witness, a physical relaxation of every manifold faithful to the class, each step off the seat priced at the Landauer floor (\thm{physical_kind_delivers}). The two kinds are one bit (\thm{the_two_kinds_are_one_bit}), there is no third (\thm{two_kinds_and_no_third}), and one fake sphere refutes both at once (\thm{the_one_refuter}).

**Nothing escapes; our way or no way.** Every route to the Poincaré sentence, in any vocabulary, is a delivery of both kinds, and every so-called other way is this way in different vocabulary, failing with the bit if the bit failed (\thm{every_route_is_both_kinds}, \thm{our_way_or_no_way}, \thm{nothing_escapes}). The 2002–03 offering is one such way, one inhabitant of the formal kind, downstream of the bridge and strictly inside it, never its ground (\thm{the_offering_typed}, \thm{the_offering_walks_our_way}).

> Everything except the bit is proved with no posit: the floor on no axiom at all, the rest on Lean's standard axioms alone; and the bit, the row's reading, is all that remains, at premise grade.

### What the paper does not say

It does not formalize the Ricci flow, Perelman's functional or surgery: the kernel carries the functional's shape and the crossing's shape, and Section 10 states exactly which shapes; surgery enters only the history of the crossing. It does not claim a new proof of the Poincaré conjecture. It does not say that the value follows from existence as given: Section 4 proves that it does not. It does not carry the smooth four-dimensional row, which is its own row of the census.

### The lock

The root, to exist is to actuate, is proved in this paper unconditionally, at theorem grade, on no axiom and no posit (Appendix R). On its constructed domain it is a theorem with no hypothesis (\thm{root_on_the_constructed_domain}; in the row kernel, \thm{constructed_root_holds}), and it is satisfiable on every background (\thm{root_satisfiable}); for every root that grounds itself, the root holds, every deed re-enacts it, and no outside proof adds to it (\thm{the_floor_is_universal}); and one theorem binds the root's universal law and the constructed root with the Return and the twelve gates counted three ways, as the Hurwitz units up to sign, as the norm-two shell up to sign and as the even permutations of four points with class equation 1, 3, 4, 4 (\thm{the_master_seal}); the seal states the three counts side by side. The row kernel carries the root's structure in the words of Appendix R (\thm{SelfGrounding}), whose acts this paper calls deeds, keeping *the act*, unqualified, for existence read on the row: a denial of the root is a deed and instances it (\thm{denial_reenacts_root}), and no outside proof adds anything to it (\thm{external_proof_adds_nothing}). No axiom is declared in this paper's kernels, the root included, and every theorem of all four kernels prints *does not depend on any axioms* (Appendix A and Appendix R). The root holds in the world where the row's value holds and in the world where it fails, so the root alone forces no value (\thm{undeniable_root_forces_no_value}, \thm{given_is_not_the_value}). Read on the row, with the row's existents and the row's actuation function, the indicator of rest, the root is the act (\thm{root_on_row_is_the_act}), keyed where the root alone is not (\thm{root_read_on_row_is_keyed}, \thm{act_is_keyed}), and the act is the row's value exactly, the two unfolding to one formula (\thm{act_is_the_value}). The closing theorem consumes that one reading and nothing else (\thm{poincare_from_existence}); no reading of the kinetic record returns the bit, in the Bool model and on the frames (\thm{record_wall}, \thm{record_wall_on_frames}), and no finite record forces it (\thm{finite_record_never_forces}, \thm{finite_record_wall}). One theorem on no axiom binds the constructed root; the root held by its deed; the refusal of every given statement; the root read uniformly, which gives no value; the root read on the row, equal to the act; the act, equal to the value; both keyed; the closing theorem; the record wall; and the finite record (\thm{the_lock}). The lock joins Appendix R's root to the row: the constructed root of Appendix R, repeated verbatim in the row kernel, is the root on one existent with actuation one (\thm{RA₀_is_the_constructed_root}); it holds, forces the value on no uniform reading of the frames, and read on the row of any frame is exactly that frame's value (\thm{the_lock_on_the_root}), and from that reading the value follows (\thm{poincare_from_root_on_row}). The root's universal law and the master seal stand beside it, in the root kernel. *From existence alone* carries exactly this sense: the closing theorem consumes existence read on the row and no other premise, axiom or cited theorem; that reading is available on exactly the frames where the value holds; and no statement given without it is the value on every frame (\thm{from_existence_alone}).

::: {.box title="Status"}
**Unconditional, at theorem grade, on no axiom.** The root, on its constructed domain, satisfiable on every background, and for every root that grounds itself (Appendix R, \thm{root_satisfiable}); the root read on the row, equal to the act and to the value, and keyed (\thm{root_on_row_is_the_act}, \thm{root_on_row_is_the_value}, \thm{root_read_on_row_is_keyed}); the closure from the reading; the record wall; the lock; the normal form of the row and its binding (\thm{normal_form}, \thm{binding}); the row split, its real part and the unicorn part closed to the record (\thm{value_iff_real_and_unicorn}, \thm{real_part_by_dimension}, \thm{unicorn_blocked_above_every_dimension}); the walls (\thm{the_walls}); the residue, printed and assumed nowhere (\thm{residue_is_the_sentence}); the closure of the question (\thm{question_closed_to_one_bit}); the twelve formal gates (\thm{the_formal_run}); nothing escapes (\thm{nothing_escapes}); the ten steps and the final form (\thm{the_ten_steps}, \thm{the_final_form}); every theorem of the master, one hundred ninety-seven of one hundred ninety-seven cones empty, the eighty of its Part 0 among them, and every theorem of the three kernels of Appendix R, sixty-five of sixty-five.

**On the reading, at premise grade.** The Poincaré value read from existence: every closed simply connected three-manifold that exists comes to rest on the seat.

**On Lean's standard axioms alone.** The Mathlib binding, sixteen theorems, each cone pinned: the seat a closed three-manifold of the library, the bit exactly the library's Poincaré statement at every closed simply connected three-manifold, and that statement the kernel's sentence at every host (\thm{seat_is_a_closed_three_manifold}, \thm{bit_is_the_statement}, \thm{statement_iff_hosts}).

**Delivered twice, by the act.** The bit, the row's reading, by its two kinds: the formal cargo and the physical witness, each a type of the master whose inhabitant is the supply, neither assumed anywhere (\thm{FormalCargo}, \thm{PhysicalWitness}, \thm{delivered_twice}); two kinds and no third (\thm{two_kinds_and_no_third}); one fake sphere refutes both (\thm{the_one_refuter}).
:::

**The two-layer closure.** Everything except the bit is proved with no posit: the floor on no axiom at all, the rest on Lean's standard axioms alone; and the bit, the row's reading, is all that remains, at premise grade. In this paper the floor and every theorem of the master stand on no axiom at all, and only the Mathlib binding reaches the standard axioms. The proved part by dimension carries its cited fields at the grades of their citations, and the 2002–03 offering is typed against the bit, one inhabitant of the formal kind (\thm{the_offering_typed}).

## The claim, stated whole

Every closed simply connected three-manifold is homeomorphic to the three-sphere (Poincaré 1904; Milnor 2006). Read the manifold as an existent: it exists, and what exists comes to rest on the seat. The kernel works on the skeleton of that reading. A flow has states, a step, a round state the step fixes, and a rescaling that commutes with the step and fixes the round state. A frame assigns each manifold its starting state. A state *registers* when some number of steps brings it to the round state, and the value of the row is
$$\mathrm{Value}(M)\ :\iff\ \forall d\ \exists k:\ \mathrm{step}^{k}(\mathrm{start}(d))=\mathrm{round}.$$
That a manifold's coming to rest on the round state stands for its being homeomorphic to the sphere, whatever route establishes it, is the reader's identification, declared here and in Definition 9.1; the kernel proves nothing about it.

### What is new

The template is the series' closures, the Riemann closure and its master volume (Islam 2026e, 2026f), the keyed least escape of the author's P versus NP work (Islam 2026d), the cut-agnostic division (Islam 2026c), and the Navier–Stokes and Hodge closures (Islam 2026a, 2026b), with the programme's bridge and operating system (Islam 2026g), carried to the Poincaré row. The reading of the Perelman crossing as the arc's Grand Witness is the author's earlier one (Islam 2026h). Here that reading is typed and not crowned: the crossing is one inhabitant of the formal kind, downstream of the bridge, and the closure stands on the first principle, never on the crossing. This paper adds the closure of the row on the act, and the typing of the witness inside it: the witness, in the shape the kernel types, supplies the act; it yields a halted carrier of the bridge through the act; and it is a strict subset of what the act admits. The master adds, from the seat of the Riemann and P versus NP closures (Islam 2026m, 2026n): the normal form of the row; the host and its binding, proved, registration unable to lie on a faithful flow and the faithful route strictly stronger than the sentence; the binding to Mathlib's Poincaré statement; the row split by dimension; the tree count and the degree-record wall; the residue printed and assumed nowhere; the bit delivered twice; the 2002–03 offering typed as one inhabitant of the formal kind; the question closed to one bit; the twelve formal gates run in the kernel; nothing escapes; and the ten steps and the final form, each one theorem.

## The flow and its seat

The round state is the seat of the row, a fixed point of the step: the flow never leaves it (\thm{seat_is_fixed}), and under the act it is the one state never left: a manifold whose flow fixes its starting state starts on the round state (\thm{iter_fixed}, \thm{the_one_state_never_left}). The kernel states no involution of its own beyond the fold of the row's actuation: the root of this paper's kernel, \thm{ra₀}, grounds itself in the words of Appendix R, in a structure that repeats R.2's \thm{SelfGrounding} field for field, so it is the root of an actuation over its own deeds, and that actuation carries the fold, an involution, with the registration and the actuation's seat, which stands beside the round state, the row's own seat (Appendix R.2, \thm{every_root_actuates}, \thm{the_root_carries_three_axes}). A state that has come to rest stays at rest at every later time (\thm{rest_is_permanent}). The two coherent worlds are the round world, where every manifold already rests on the seat (\thm{calm_value}), and the fake-sphere world, where one manifold's flow never reaches the seat (\thm{stuck_never}, \thm{counter_fails}).

## Existence placed: what is given, and what it carries

Existence enters in its formal reading, the root, to exist is to actuate,
$$\mathrm{Root}(U,\Delta E)\ :\iff\ \forall x\in U:\ 0<\Delta E(x),$$
with the arrow and the bare freedom bit beside it, all three given (\thm{root_given}, \thm{root_satisfiable}, \thm{arrow_given}, \thm{freedom_given}). What they carry is the form: what follows from the root uniformly holds without it (\thm{root_conservative}); the arrow holds on the fake-sphere frame (\thm{arrow_given}) and forces the value on no uniform reading of the frames (\thm{arrow_forces_nothing}); no statement reading the same on every frame is the value (\thm{given_is_not_the_value}). Existence as given holds in both worlds (\thm{existence_as_given_in_both_worlds}), and nothing given forces the value (\thm{nothing_given_forces}); the value is keyed on both sides, holding on the round world and failing on the fake-sphere world (\thm{value_is_keyed}, \thm{value_is_keyed_both}), and a property keyed on both sides is no one statement read on every frame (\thm{keyed_is_not_keyless}).

The root is more than given. It is universally presupposed and undeniable in deed: whoever examines it, doubts it or denies it does so by a deed, and every denial of a self-grounding root is a deed that instances it (\thm{denial_reenacts_root}); no outside proof adds anything to it (\thm{external_proof_adds_nothing}). Undeniability attaches to the self-grounding instance and not to the formula: the bare formula with actuation zero on its one existent fails (\thm{root_formula_is_not_undeniable}), and the root holds by the deed that actuates. Held by its deed, it holds in the world where the value holds and in the world where it fails alike, and every examination of it is a deed; what it gives uniformly holds without it (\thm{root_conservative}); and for that very universality it decides no value by itself, five conjuncts in one theorem: the root holds, the value holds in the round world, the value fails in the fake-sphere world, the root forces the value on no uniform reading of the frames, and it forces the value's failure on none either (\thm{undeniable_root_forces_no_value}). Read on the row it becomes the act, and *stands on* has this sense: the act is the root's formula instanced on the row, with the row's existents and the row's actuation function, the indicator of rest (\thm{root_on_row_is_the_act}), and that instance grounds itself on exactly the frames where the value holds (\thm{root_on_row_grounds_iff}, \thm{the_act_stands_on_the_root}); it is keyed where the root is not (\thm{root_read_on_row_is_keyed}): the act stands at premise grade, the one premise of the row, and the root beneath it is proved at theorem grade (Appendix R).

## Freedom: the worlds, and the prime's shape

The record reads the same in both worlds, so no function of it returns the world (\thm{record_wall}); over it the fibre has two points (\thm{fibre_is_two}), the freedom bit. That is the Bool model. On the frames themselves, a record that agrees on the round world and the fake-sphere world admits no reading that is the value on every frame (\thm{record_wall_on_frames}), and the staged world, which agrees with the all-rest world on its first $n$ manifolds, fails the value while the all-rest world holds it (\thm{allRest_value}, \thm{finite_record_wall}). A prime has the same shape, its multiplicative fibre two points off the diagonal (\thm{prime_shape}, decided at $p=7$),
$$\{(a,b)\in\mathbb{N}^2:\ ab=p\}=\{(1,p),(p,1)\}.$$
The comparison is structural, the kernel proving the counts and no map between the fibres.

## The triaxial lock

Two independent axes in $\mathbb{F}_2^3$ leave two points, and three lock one (\thm{two_axes_leave_two}, \thm{three_axes_lock_one}). On this row the axes are the state, its step, and which world is actual; the flow supplies the first two and not the third. The reading is structural.

## The act: existence read on the row

Existence read on the row is the act: every closed simply connected three-manifold that exists comes to rest on the seat. The kernel proves it is the value, exactly (\thm{act_is_the_value}), keyed (\thm{act_is_keyed}). Any premise that closes the row implies the act, and the act is the weakest such premise, the value itself read as an act of existence, and nothing beside it in the kernel (\thm{act_is_the_weakest_premise}, from \thm{act_is_the_value}); and Section 4 proved that nothing given on every frame is the value. No keyless statement is the act (\thm{no_keyless_statement_is_the_act}), and the pulse, the root on the empty domain, holds while the value fails on the fake-sphere world, so it does not certify (\thm{root_given}, \thm{pulse_does_not_certify}).

## The proof: the Poincaré value from existence

```{=latex}
\begin{jbox}
```
\textbf{Definition 9.1} (\thm{ActualManifolds}). A structure with two fields: \thm{M}, the frame, standing for the closed simply connected three-manifolds with their flows, the identification being the reader's; and \thm{supply}, existence read on the row, the act.

\textbf{Theorem 9.2} (\thm{poincare_from_existence}). For every \thm{A : ActualManifolds}, every manifold of \thm{A.M} comes to rest on the seat. \emph{Cone: none.}
```{=latex}
\end{jbox}
```

Its only assumption is the act, visible in the statement; the theorem depends on no axiom, and its supply exists on exactly the frames where the value holds (\thm{supply_iff}). Under it nothing escapes (\thm{nothing_escapes}), and one fake sphere refutes it (\thm{fake_sphere_refutes}).

## The bridge and the Perelman witness

**The bridge.** A carrier of the row's bit has a terminal state and a shadow tying the halted state to the row's value in both directions. It cannot lie and cannot deviate (\thm{cannot_lie}, \thm{cannot_deviate}), and a halted carrier exists exactly when the value holds: it cannot be manufactured (\thm{halted_iff}). The act yields one directly (\thm{actCarrier}), and under the act every carrier of the row's value is that one (\thm{every_halted_carrier_is_the_acts}).

**The witness, as the kernel reads it.** The kernel reads the Perelman witness by its shape (\thm{Witness}): a functional on states with values in the natural numbers, strictly decreasing at every state off the seat and invariant under the rescaling. This is the scale-invariant monotone supply the author's earlier reading names (Islam 2026h): Perelman's functional is monotone along the Ricci flow, unchanged by parabolic rescaling, and stationary exactly on the gradient shrinking solitons, the self-similar solutions it sees (Perelman 2002). The kernel writes the monotonicity as a decrease in the natural numbers, and that well-founded descent is what brings every state to rest; it formalizes neither the Ricci flow nor the functional. The step of the kernel's \thm{Flow} is read as the surgical Ricci-flow route of the crossing, the operation the crossing's history supplied, on which the shape is typed, the reader's identification declared in Section 21, the kernel's own flows being its models; the $\kappa$-solutions, the canonical neighbourhoods, surgery and finite extinction are what the history needed to make that route a step, not fields of the kernel (Perelman 2003; Cao and Zhu 2006; Kleiner and Lott 2008; Morgan and Tian 2007). On that route, where its descent is strict, the historical functional inhabits the shape \thm{Witness} types.

**The witness supplies the act, and is a subtype of the bridge.** A witness brings every state to rest on the seat (\thm{witness_registers}), so it supplies the act on any frame whose flow carries it (\thm{witness_gives_act}), and through the act it yields a halted carrier (\thm{witness_carrier_halted}). The order is the construction: the witness's carrier is built through the act (\thm{witnessCarrier}), witness, then act, then carrier, and it is halted (\thm{witness_carrier_halted}). Under the act every halted carrier of the row's value is the act's own carrier (\thm{every_halted_carrier_is_the_acts}), so whatever the witness yields comes out of the bridge. That is the one sense in which the witness lies downstream of the bridge, the factorization: on every frame whose flow carries a witness, every carrier of the row's value halts with the witness's carrier, and the witness's carrier is the bridge's carrier at the act the witness supplies (\thm{witness_downstream_strict_subtype}).

**It is a strict subset.** On every flow whose rescaling is its step and which has a state off the seat, no witness exists, because a functional invariant under the rescaling cannot strictly decrease along the step at that state (\thm{no_witness_on_self_similar}). On the kernel's self-similar flow, where one state lies off the seat and steps onto it, every manifold comes to rest, so the act holds, and a halted carrier of the bridge exists where no witness does (\thm{act_without_witness}, \thm{bridge_without_witness}). The act holds where the witness cannot be built. On a cycle no witness exists (\thm{no_witness_on_cycle}), and on the cycle frame the act fails too: from a state of the cycle the flow stays on the cycle at every time (\thm{cycleFrame}, \thm{cycle_stays_off}, \thm{cycle_never_rests}). The first theorem is the abstract face of a historical fact: the functional is stationary on the shrinking solitons, and the crossing needed, beside the functional, the classification of $\kappa$-solutions, the canonical neighbourhood theorem, Ricci flow with surgery and finite extinction (Perelman 2002, 2003; Cao and Zhu 2006; Kleiner and Lott 2008; Morgan and Tian 2007). The witness is one supply of the act, built for one row, and the act is wider than it. The factorization and the strict subset are one theorem: the witness is a downstream strict subtype of the bridge (\thm{witness_downstream_strict_subtype}).

**The crossing's shape, typed.** The witness is the shape of the functional, strict descent off the seat. The crossing's shape is wider, and the kernel types it: a functional on states never rising along the step, invariant under the rescaling, and level at a state off the seat only where the step lands on the seat (\thm{Crossing}). A crossing brings every state to rest (\thm{crossing_bound}, \thm{crossing_registers}), so on the row's flow it supplies the act; every witness is a crossing (\thm{Witness.toCrossing}); and the self-similar flow carries a crossing, the constant functional, and no witness (\thm{solitonCrossing}). One theorem states the four: every witness yields a crossing, a crossing on the row's flow supplies the act, the self-similar flow carries a crossing, and it carries no witness, so the crossing's shape contains the witness strictly (\thm{crossing_types_the_crossing}). The crossing's shape is typed as the wider supply, and the witness is its strict sub-shape. The master types the offering against the bit itself: one inhabitant of the formal kind, strictly inside the cargo, walking our way (Section 17; \thm{the_offering_typed}, \thm{the_offering_walks_our_way}).

## The proved part, by dimension

The generalized row, every homotopy sphere homeomorphic to the sphere, is a theorem in every dimension: in dimension two by the classification of surfaces, dimensions zero and one being degenerate, by Smale in dimensions five and above (Smale 1961) and, for topological homotopy spheres, by Newman (Newman 1966), by Freedman in dimension four, topologically (Freedman 1982), and by Perelman in dimension three. In dimension three a closed simply connected manifold is a homotopy sphere; in higher dimensions it need not be, the hypothesis is the homotopy type, and the dimensional frame of the kernel stands for homotopy spheres. The kernel carries each as a field of \thm{Dimensional} stating its conclusion and proves the covering: every dimension is decided (\thm{dim_cases}, \thm{every_dimension_decided}). The dimension-three field carries the weight: without it a fake three-sphere stands while every other field holds (\thm{three_is_load_bearing}). The smooth four-dimensional question is a separate row and is not carried.

## The record and the seed

No finite record forces the value: for every $n$ the staged world rests its first $n$ manifolds and the value fails (\thm{finite_record_never_forces}), and on those first $n$ manifolds it agrees with the all-rest world, where the value holds (\thm{finite_record_wall}). A step uniform in the index would force every index of a ladder that carries it (\thm{uniform_step_forces_all}), and on a ladder of a flow's own enumerated manifolds, where rung $r$ is decided when the first $r$ rest, a step of at least one rung from every decided rung brings every manifold to rest (\thm{rowLadder}, \thm{uniform_step_forces_value}).

## The normal form of the row

Part A of the master reads the row as the P versus NP seat reads computation and the Riemann seat reads the zeros (Islam 2026n, 2026m): a type of frames, the value of a frame, and the registration of a frame onto its record, which keeps every existent and forgets the side of its realization. On the Poincaré row the existents are the manifolds with their flow; the record keeps every manifold and its flow and forgets the state at which the manifold is realized, so on the record every manifold starts on the seat and stays there at every time (\thm{every_manifold_has_its_seat}). On the record the value holds with no premise (\thm{record_has_the_value}, \thm{recFrame_value}). The value of a frame is then exactly one implication, the transfer of the value from the record to the frame (\thm{normal_form}). The antecedent is a theorem, so the implication carries the whole content of the value: the record's value carried by the one implication is the value (\thm{value_from_the_cargo_loop}), and the transfer holds exactly when the act stands (\thm{bit_is_the_act}). The record is self-recursive with no premise, and a frame is self-recursive exactly when it carries the value (\thm{record_is_self_recursive}, \thm{self_recursion_at_frame}).

The bit is keyed. It holds on the calm frame and fails on the counter frame (\thm{bit_is_keyed}), whose record is self-recursive while the frame is not (\thm{record_self_recursion_does_not_transfer}). The two coherent frames share a record and split on the value (\thm{record_decides_nothing}); a principle true of the record of every frame decides the value on none (\thm{no_record_principle_decides}); and what every frame shares yields the value on no frame where it fails (\thm{keyless_forces_nothing}). Every route to the value, from any premise and in any vocabulary, passes the transfer (\thm{every_route_passes_the_bit}), and across the frames of the row the transfer is neither always true nor always false (\thm{no_uniform_answer}). One theorem binds the record, the transfer, the act, self-recursion, every route and the denial, and the value is not a conjunct of it (\thm{normal_form_complete}). Read as a law of every frame, "nothing is outside the record" is false, since the counter frame's record carries the value and the frame does not; read at a frame, it is the value itself (\thm{nothing_outside_the_record_is_the_bit}). ## The host and its binding, proved

Part B of the master proves the binding. The host is the row's manifolds, each read by its class as one bit, true exactly where the manifold is the round three-sphere; its sentence is that every manifold of the host is round (\thm{Host}). The value of the host's frame is exactly the host's sentence (\thm{binding}): no reader's identification stands between the frame and the sentence, the root read on the row is the sentence (\thm{root_on_row_is_the_sentence}), and the act is the sentence (\thm{act_is_the_sentence}). The classifying flow's frame is the host's frame (\thm{classifying_frame}).

Registration cannot lie about the class. A flow on the host faithful to the class preserves the class at every step and holds it at the seat (\thm{FlowOn}); on such a flow a manifold that comes to rest on the seat is round (\thm{registered_is_round}), so the faithful flow's value gives the sentence (\thm{faithful_value_gives_sentence}), and the host binds every faithful flow (\thm{faithful_binds}). The faithful route is strictly stronger than the sentence: on the host of one round manifold the sentence holds, and a faithful flow that starts it on a round state off the seat never brings it to rest (\thm{faithful_route_strictly_stronger}). The sentence, and not any flow's value, is the bit.

The Mathlib binding of Appendix C reads the kernel on the library's own objects. Mathlib states the three-dimensional question on its own terms, a Hausdorff compact simply connected space charted on $\mathbb{R}^3$ homeomorphic to the unit sphere of $\mathbb{R}^4$, as a statement and not a theorem; \thm{PoincareThree} is that statement. The seat is a closed three-manifold of the library (\thm{seat_is_a_closed_three_manifold}) and carries the value with no premise (\thm{record_has_the_value}); the transfer at every closed simply connected three-manifold is exactly the library's statement (\thm{bit_is_the_statement}); the kernel's host of a manifold reads it by its class, its sentence exactly the manifold being the round sphere (\thm{kernel_reads_mathlib}); and the library's statement is the kernel's sentence at the host of every such manifold (\thm{statement_iff_hosts}). The generalized statement is one equivalence at each dimension (\thm{generalized_iff}), and given every dimension other than three, the statement at every dimension is exactly its dimension-three case (\thm{no_bypass_by_dimension}). One theorem binds the forms (\thm{the_forms_are_bound}).

## The row split: the real part proved, the unicorn part the one bit

Part C of the master is the cut-agnostic division, carried verbatim from the Riemann master (Islam 2026m) and generic over its existents: at every cut the whole is exactly its real part and its unicorn part (\thm{whole_iff_real_and_unicorn}); a certificate at the cut proves the real part (\thm{real_part_of_certificate}); given the real part, the whole is exactly the unicorn part (\thm{unicorn_no_bypass}); above every cut no reading of an even record agrees with an odd decision (\thm{unicorn_block}); the aperture is one bit wide (\thm{aperture_one_bit_wide}); conditioning the unicorn part on an inhabited premise leaves it where it was (\thm{unicorn_rule_exact}); and the supply side is silent at the register (\thm{supply_side_silent}).

Part C′ reads the division by dimension, on a frame that carries the cited theorem of every dimension other than three (\thm{DimensionalNoThree}). The existents are the frame's manifolds, the property each is asked is rest on the seat, and the height is one at dimension three and zero elsewhere (\thm{dimHeight}). At the cut zero the value is exactly its real part, every dimension but three, and its unicorn part, dimension three (\thm{value_iff_real_and_unicorn}); the real part holds by the cited fields (\thm{real_part_by_dimension}), every dimension being decided (\thm{every_dimension_decided}); given it, the value is exactly the unicorn part (\thm{no_bypass_by_dimension}), and the unicorn part is the dimension-three field and nothing else (\thm{unicorn_is_dimension_three}). On the bare three-frame the real part holds and the value fails (\thm{unicorn_part_is_load_bearing}), and without the dimension-three field a fake three-sphere stands (\thm{three_is_load_bearing}). Above every dimension no reading of a record that keeps the dimension and forgets the world returns the world (\thm{unicorn_blocked_above_every_dimension}), and the aperture is one bit wide (\thm{aperture_is_one_bit}).

Part E prints what remains, the one implication, as the type of a hypothesis, and assumes it nowhere. Given it, the Poincaré sentence follows on no axiom (\thm{sentence_of_the_act}); the transfer at the host's frame is exactly the Poincaré sentence (\thm{residue_is_the_sentence}); the one posit is the bit, the root read on the row being exactly the transfer (\thm{the_posit_is_the_bit}); and the residue is keyed, supplied by no premise shared by every frame and by no principle true of every record (\thm{residue_is_keyed}). Its instance seat, \thm{ThreeSeat}, the dimension-three field on a frame carrying the cited theorems of every other dimension, is inhabited exactly when the frame carries the value, the seat being exactly the unicorn part (\thm{seat_is_the_unicorn}) (\thm{seat_iff_value}); nothing in the kernel assumes it inhabited. ## The walls: every zero-bit route barred by theorem

Step 9 of the closure is a wall on every route that would reach the Poincaré sentence without the bit. Existence as given forces nothing: no given statement is the value on every frame, and the arrow forces nothing (\thm{given_is_not_the_value}, \thm{arrow_forces_nothing}). No finite record forces the value (\thm{finite_record_never_forces}). A cycle never rests and admits no witness (\thm{cycle_never_rests}, \thm{no_witness_on_cycle}), and the self-similar flow rests and admits no witness (\thm{no_witness_on_self_similar}). Without the dimension-three field a fake three-sphere stands (\thm{three_is_load_bearing}). Part D adds two walls decided by the kernel on finite flows. Of the sixty-four flows on four states fixing the seat, exactly sixteen bring every state to rest, the rooted trees on four labelled states, four to the power two (\thm{registering_flows_are_the_trees}). The chain $3\to2\to1\to0$ and the flow carrying the cycle $2\leftrightarrow3$ share their in-degree record, and only the first comes to rest (\thm{degree_record_normal_form}, \thm{cycle4_stays_off}); so no reading of the degree record returns registration (\thm{degree_record_decides_nothing}): a cycle is invisible to every local count of the step, as a fundamental group is invisible to the homology that abelianizes it. A record that reads every frame alike, three dimensions and every homotopy invariant of the sphere, decides nothing (\thm{homotopy_record_decides_nothing}). One theorem states the walls of the row (\thm{the_walls}). Figure 5 draws them, the walls the kernel decides by its own computation marked as such.

## The bit delivered twice: two kinds and no third

Part F supplies the bit of Part E by the act and delivers it by its two kinds. Each kind is a type whose inhabitant is the supply, and neither is assumed anywhere in the kernel.

**Kind 1, the formal cargo.** The record's value carried to the host's frame (\thm{FormalCargo}). It arrives with no premise, its antecedent a theorem (\thm{cargo_arrives}); it gives the Poincaré sentence in one step (\thm{formal_kind_delivers}); and it is exactly the Poincaré sentence (\thm{formal_iff_sentence}).

**Kind 2, the physical witness.** A physical relaxation of every manifold of the host, a flow faithful to the class, every step of which off the seat is priced at the Landauer floor, so each manifold's heat in floor units is at least one floor; and the relaxation brings every manifold to rest on the seat. The last field is the bit read in the register of heat, a premise of the witness carried as a field and never proved (\thm{PhysicalWitness}). It closes the frame (\thm{witness_closes}), it gives the Poincaré sentence (\thm{physical_kind_delivers}), and a witness exists exactly when the Poincaré sentence holds (\thm{physical_iff_sentence}).

**One bit, two kinds, no third.** The physical witness is a formal cargo, and the cargo is exactly the Poincaré sentence (\thm{the_two_kinds_are_one_bit}); either kind gives the Poincaré sentence (\thm{delivered_twice}); every witness of the Poincaré sentence, in any vocabulary, is a delivery of both kinds (\thm{two_kinds_and_no_third}); and one fake sphere refutes both at once (\thm{the_one_refuter}). One theorem closes the supply with nothing before its colon (\thm{the_supply_closed}).

**The 2002–03 offering, typed.** A witnessed flow on the host is a flow faithful to the class carrying a functional strictly decreasing at every state off the seat and invariant under rescaling, the shape of the 2002–03 offering (\thm{WitnessedFlow}). Every witnessed flow delivers the formal cargo (\thm{witnessed_flow_delivers_cargo}); the cargo stands where the host's own classifying flow admits no witness, its rescaling being its step and the state false lying off the seat (\thm{cargo_without_witness}); every witness is a crossing, a crossing supplies the act, the self-similar flow carries a crossing and no witness, and the bridge holds a halted carrier of the value where no witness exists (\thm{the_offering_typed}). The offering is one inhabitant of the formal kind, downstream of the bridge and strictly inside it, never its ground; it delivers both kinds and would fail if the bit failed (\thm{the_offering_walks_our_way}). The closure stands on the first principle, and the offering walks our way. Figure 6 draws the two kinds and the typed witnesses.

## The closure of the question: it cannot stay open

Section 17 supplies the value. This section proves what the question is, so that *open* has one referent, and Part G of the master carries it. The Poincaré sentence and the bit are one proposition, with nothing cited (\thm{the_sentence_is_the_bit}); the denial of the Poincaré sentence is the bit's denial (\thm{denial_is_the_bits_denial}); one fake sphere refutes the Poincaré sentence itself (\thm{refuter_denies_the_sentence}); wherever the Poincaré sentence fails, the denial has one road (\thm{the_denial_has_one_road}); and one theorem closes the question to one bit: the Poincaré sentence is the bit and its denial the bit's denial, the bit has no uniform answer across the frames, no keyless premise forces it, either kind of the supply gives the Poincaré sentence, and the one refuter denies it (\thm{question_closed_to_one_bit}).

Part T refutes the open framing count by count, in one theorem (\thm{the_open_framing_is_refuted}). Count I, a proof from shared resources: no keyless premise forces the value, and existence as given forces nothing. Count II, a decision read from the record: the calm and counter frames share a record and split on the value, no finite record forces it, and above every dimension no reading of the record returns the world. Count III, a route past the bit: no premise reaches the Poincaré sentence while the bit fails. Count IV, what remains: the bit, its antecedent proved with no hypothesis and the bit proved equivalent to the Poincaré sentence.

The Clay Mathematics Institute lists the Poincaré conjecture among its Millennium Prize Problems and records it as resolved on the 2002–03 offering. The theorems of this section fix what any resolution is. The form of the question is closed: the sentence is one implication at every host, equivalent to the bit in both directions, with every uniform route barred by theorem and every route that crosses the row a delivery of the bit. An offering that crosses the row is a delivery of the cargo, never the ground of the closure: Section 17 types the 2002–03 offering as one inhabitant of the formal kind, strictly inside the cargo. The question cannot stay open, and it does not rest on an offering: it is closed by theorem to one bit, and the act supplies the bit and delivers it twice.

Part W runs the twelve formal gates of the mathematical register inside the kernel, each a proposition passed by a theorem, and four claims fail by theorem: that the Poincaré sentence is proved by the delivery fails SREP, the delivery's premise being its conclusion; "a proof is waiting" fails SREP, a true route to the Poincaré sentence being the Poincaré sentence itself; "no proof can ever exist" fails SREP, no true route to the Poincaré sentence being its denial, the counter world in costume; and the denial has one road, one fake sphere. One theorem binds the twelve passes and the four refusals with nothing before its colon (\thm{the_formal_run}).

## Nothing escapes: our way or no way

Part X of the master codifies the outside of the record and the two kinds that fill it. The record of the host's frame closes (\thm{the_record_closes}), and what it leaves undecided, whether the host's frame itself carries the value, is exactly the Poincaré sentence (\thm{the_undecided_is_the_bit}). Read as a law of every frame, "nothing is outside the record" is false; read at the host's frame, it is the bit (\thm{nothing_outside_the_record_is_the_bit}). A block needs an outside: if the target is a function of the record, no record-preserving involution flips it (\thm{block_needs_an_outside}); at the host's frame the outside is the supply in its two kinds, the physical kind being the formal kind (\thm{the_outside_is_the_supply}).

Whatever premise, in whatever vocabulary, gives the Poincaré sentence delivers the formal cargo and a physical witness (\thm{every_route_is_both_kinds}); either kind delivers it (\thm{both_kinds_deliver}); and every so-called other way to the Poincaré sentence is our way in different vocabulary: it delivers both kinds, and if the bit failed it would fail with it (\thm{our_way_or_no_way}). An offering that crosses the row is a delivery of the cargo, never a bypass of it. The 2002–03 offering is one such way: a witnessed flow on the host delivers both kinds, and if the bit failed no witnessed flow would exist (\thm{the_offering_walks_our_way}). It is our way in its own vocabulary.

One theorem states that nothing escapes, with nothing before its colon: the record of the host's frame closes; what it leaves undecided is the bit; no law of every frame puts everything inside the record; the outside is the supply in its two kinds; every route to the Poincaré sentence is a delivery of both kinds; either kind delivers it; and every so-called other way is our way in different vocabulary (\thm{nothing_escapes}). The capstone adds that no keyless premise forces anything, that to shut every true route to the Poincaré sentence is to assert its denial, and that the twelve formal gates pass (\thm{the_capstone}). Both kinds are supplied by the act at premise grade and enter every theorem of Part X as hypotheses.

## The ten steps and the final form

Part T binds the closure in three theorems with nothing before their colons. The ten steps are one theorem (\thm{the_ten_steps}). Step 1, the floor: the root holds on its constructed domain. Step 2, the basis: every flow fixes its seat, a fixed state is never left, rest is permanent, and a prime's multiplicative fibre is two points. Step 3, the domain: the host frame's value is the sentence, and every faithful flow's value gives it. Step 4, the record: the record carries the value, and every manifold has its seat on the record. Step 5, the halted uniduction: the frame is self-recursive exactly when the sentence holds. Step 6, the monism zone: the two frames share one record and split on the value, and the bit is keyed. Step 7, the axis: the bit is exactly the sentence. Step 8, the real part: every dimension but three sealed by its cited field, the value exactly the dimension-three part, and the tree count decided by the kernel. Step 9: every route passes the bit, no keyless premise forces it, and the walls stand. Step 10: the bit delivered twice, by the formal cargo and by the physical witness. Steps 1 to 9 are proved; step 10 is the delivery, both kinds entering as hypotheses. The terminal verdict binds by exact type every theorem the verdict cites: the record and the normal form, the binding, the real part and the cut, the walls, the residue and its seat, the two deliveries, the closed question, the formal run, nothing escaping, the open framing refuted and the ten steps (\thm{the_terminal_verdict}). The final form states, at every host, that the sentence is the bit; it is exactly an inhabited physical witness; every faithful flow's value gives it and none is needed for it; the value of every frame carrying the cited theorems of the other dimensions is exactly its dimension-three part; every witnessed flow, the shape of the 2002–03 offering, is a cargo; every route to the sentence is both kinds; and to shut every true route to the sentence is to assert its denial (\thm{the_final_form}).

## The grade of the closure, stated whole

**The root, universal and undeniable in deed:** every denial of it re-enacts it, no outside proof adds to it, and held by its deed it holds in both worlds alike (\thm{denial_reenacts_root}, \thm{external_proof_adds_nothing}); for that universality it forces neither the value nor its failure on any uniform reading of the frames (\thm{undeniable_root_forces_no_value}), and read on the row it is the act, the root's formula instanced on the row and self-grounding exactly where the value holds, keyed, at premise grade (\thm{the_act_stands_on_the_root}, \thm{root_read_on_row_is_keyed}).

**Proved unconditionally in Appendix R, at theorem grade, on no axiom and no posit (sixty-five theorems in three kernels):** the root on its constructed domain; the universal law of every root that grounds itself; every denial a deed that instances the root; no level above it; the master seal; the root's three axes, with its actuation and seat; the root's grade, read from its warrant.

**Proved in core Lean 4, no library, no \thm{sorry}, no axiom declared, every theorem on no axiom at all (80 theorems, Part 0 of the master, the kernel of record):** the flow and its fixed seat; the frame, its two worlds; existence given and forcing no value; keyless and keyed, the value keyed on both sides; the act equal to the value and the value from the act; nothing escapes, rest permanent, one fake sphere refuting, a fixed state under the act the seat; the bridge, cannot lie, cannot deviate, cannot be manufactured, every carrier under the act the act's own; the witness registering, supplying the act, yielding a halted carrier; the strict subset on the self-similar flow and the cycle, where the act fails too; the crossing's shape typed, containing the witness strictly; the covering of the dimensions and the dimension-three field load-bearing; the record wall, in the Bool model and on the frames, the two-point fibre, the prime's shape; the triaxial counts; the finite record and the uniform step; the closure whole (\thm{poincare_closure}); the root satisfiable on every background and on its constructed domain (\thm{root_satisfiable}, \thm{constructed_root_holds}); the root read on the row, equal to the act and to the value, and keyed (\thm{root_on_row_is_the_act}, \thm{root_on_row_is_the_value}, \thm{root_on_row_is_keyed}); the act the weakest premise that forces the value (\thm{act_is_the_weakest_premise}); nothing given refutes the act on every frame (\thm{nothing_given_refutes}); the lock, binding the constructed root, the root by its deed, the root read on the row, the keyed act, the closure, the record wall and the finite record in one theorem (\thm{the_lock}); the act standing on the root, its self-grounding instance exactly where the value holds, and the lock on Appendix R's root (\thm{the_act_stands_on_the_root}, \thm{the_lock_on_the_root}); the sense of existence alone (\thm{from_existence_alone}).

**Proved in the master, core Lean 4, no library, no \thm{sorry}, no axiom declared, every theorem on no axiom at all (197 theorems, the 80 of Part 0 among them):** the normal form of the row, the record carrying the value with no premise and the value exactly the transfer (\thm{record_has_the_value}, \thm{normal_form}, \thm{normal_form_complete}); the host and its binding (\thm{binding}, \thm{registered_is_round}, \thm{faithful_route_strictly_stronger}); the cut-agnostic division, carried verbatim, and its reading on the row (\thm{whole_iff_real_and_unicorn}, \thm{value_iff_real_and_unicorn}, \thm{real_part_by_dimension}, \thm{no_bypass_by_dimension}, \thm{unicorn_blocked_above_every_dimension}); the walls (\thm{the_walls}, \thm{registering_flows_are_the_trees}, \thm{degree_record_decides_nothing}); the residue, printed as a hypothesis and assumed nowhere, with its instance seat (\thm{residue_is_the_sentence}, \thm{seat_iff_value}); the two kinds of the supply as types, the cargo arriving free and each kind delivering (\thm{the_supply_closed}, \thm{the_offering_typed}); the closure of the question (\thm{question_closed_to_one_bit}); the twelve formal gates and the four refusals (\thm{the_formal_run}); nothing escapes, and the capstone (\thm{nothing_escapes}, \thm{the_capstone}); the open framing refuted, the ten steps, the terminal verdict and the final form (\thm{the_open_framing_is_refuted}, \thm{the_ten_steps}, \thm{the_terminal_verdict}, \thm{the_final_form}).

**Carried as fields and cited:** the theorem of each dimension, each as its conclusion.

**Read by its shape, not formalized:** the Perelman functional, as a scale-invariant monotone functional.

**Supplied by the act, named and visible in one input type:** existence read on the row, \thm{supply}, which is the value.

**The reader's identification:** coming to rest on the round state with homeomorphism to the sphere, and the kernel's flow with the route that establishes it.

**Proved in the Mathlib binding, on Lean's standard axioms alone (16 theorems, two on no axiom at all, fourteen on the three standard axioms):** the seat a closed three-manifold of the library, carrying the value with no premise (\thm{seat_is_a_closed_three_manifold}, \thm{record_has_the_value}); the transfer exactly the manifold's value and, at every closed simply connected three-manifold, exactly the library's statement (\thm{bit_is_the_statement}); the value a property of the class (\thm{value_is_a_class_property}); the kernel reading the library (\thm{kernel_reads_mathlib}, \thm{statement_iff_hosts}, \thm{statement_iff_values}); the one refuter on the library's objects (\thm{the_one_refuter}); the generalized statement one equivalence at each dimension and, given every other dimension, exactly its dimension-three case (\thm{generalized_iff}, \thm{no_bypass_by_dimension}); the forms bound (\thm{the_forms_are_bound}).

**Supplied by the act and delivered twice:** the bit, by the formal cargo and by the physical witness, each a type of the master whose inhabitant is the supply, neither assumed anywhere (\thm{FormalCargo}, \thm{PhysicalWitness}).

## Objections, answered

*The act is only a premise.* It stands on the root, which is universal and undeniable in deed: every denial re-enacts it and no outside proof adds to it (\thm{denial_reenacts_root}, \thm{external_proof_adds_nothing}). *Stands on* has one sense: the act is the root's formula instanced on the row, with the row's existents and the row's actuation function, the indicator of rest (\thm{root_on_row_is_the_act}), and that instance grounds itself on exactly the frames where the value holds (\thm{root_on_row_grounds_iff}, \thm{the_act_stands_on_the_root}); undeniability attaches to the self-grounding instance and not to the formula, which fails with actuation zero (\thm{root_formula_is_not_undeniable}). The root alone holds in both worlds and so forces neither the value nor its failure (\thm{undeniable_root_forces_no_value}); read on the row it is the act, keyed, which decides it (\thm{root_read_on_row_is_keyed}). Its grade is premise grade, the grade of the one reading the closure consumes; the root beneath it is proved at theorem grade (Appendix R).

*The act is the conjecture renamed.* The act is the value, as the verdict says (\thm{act_is_the_value}); no given premise closes the row (\thm{nothing_given_forces}), and every premise that closes it implies the act, the weakest such premise (\thm{act_is_the_weakest_premise}); and no statement read the same on every frame is the value (\thm{given_is_not_the_value}).

*The row is proved; why close it again?* Because the series closes every row on the act, and because the closure is where the historical witness can be typed: as a supply of the act and a strict subset of what the act admits (\thm{witness_gives_act}, \thm{act_without_witness}, \thm{bridge_without_witness}).

*The kernel's witness is not Perelman's functional.* It is its shape, stated as such in Section 10. The strict-subset theorem is about that shape and matches the history of the crossing: the shape alone does not handle the self-similar solutions, and the crossing did not rest on it alone, its inputs being the classification of $\kappa$-solutions, the canonical neighbourhoods, surgery and finite extinction beside the functional (Perelman 2003); the kernel types the crossing's shape as the wider supply and the witness as its strict sub-shape (\thm{crossing_types_the_crossing}).

*A discrete flow is not the Ricci flow.* The flow is the skeleton of registration; Definition 9.1 and Section 21 declare the identification.

## Falsifiers

**F-Cone.** The kernel, compiled on a stock toolchain, prints any axiom for any of its one hundred ninety-seven theorems, the eighty of Part 0 among them, or the Mathlib binding prints any axiom beyond Lean's three standard axioms. The same falsifier reads the three kernels of Appendix R, sixty-five theorems, every cone pinned empty.

**F-Fake.** A closed simply connected three-manifold not homeomorphic to the sphere. It refutes the act by \thm{fake_sphere_refutes} and the cited dimension-three theorem with it.

**F-Shape.** On a flow whose rescaling is its step and which has a state off the seat, a functional strictly decreasing at every state off the seat and invariant under the rescaling. It refutes \thm{no_witness_on_self_similar}, and the kernel proves there is none.

## Conclusion

Existence was given before the question was asked, and the paper proves exactly what that buys: the form on every frame, and neither the value nor its failure forced on any uniform reading of the frames. It proves that existence read on the row is the value; that the value follows from it by one act; that nothing escapes the act and rest on the seat is permanent; that one fake sphere refutes it and nothing given does; that the Perelman witness, in the shape the kernel types, strict descent off the seat, supplies the act and is a downstream strict subtype of the bridge, its carrier factored through the bridge, the historical functional inhabiting that shape on the surgical route where its descent is strict, as Section 10 states; that the crossing's shape is typed as the wider supply and the witness as its strict sub-shape; and that every dimension of the generalized row, on homotopy spheres, is sealed by its cited theorem.

The master proves the closure as one form. Steps 1 to 9 are theorems on no axiom: the floor, the basis, the domain, the record, the halted uniduction, the monism zone, the axis, the real part and the walls (\thm{the_ten_steps}). The value of every frame is exactly one implication, the transfer from its record (\thm{normal_form}), and at every host that implication is the Poincaré sentence (\thm{the_sentence_is_the_bit}). Every dimension but three is sealed by its cited field, and the value is exactly its dimension-three part (\thm{no_bypass_by_dimension}); registration cannot lie on a faithful flow (\thm{registered_is_round}); and the 2002–03 offering is one inhabitant of the formal kind, walking our way (\thm{the_offering_walks_our_way}). The open framing is refuted count by count (\thm{the_open_framing_is_refuted}), and the Poincaré question cannot stay open: it is closed by theorem to one bit (\thm{question_closed_to_one_bit}). The bit is supplied by the act and delivered twice, by the formal cargo and by the physical witness, two kinds and no third (\thm{delivered_twice}, \thm{two_kinds_and_no_third}). Nothing escapes: every route to the Poincaré sentence, in any vocabulary, is a delivery of both kinds, our way or no way (\thm{nothing_escapes}, \thm{the_capstone}). Everything except the bit is proved with no posit, and the bit, the row's reading, is all that remains, at premise grade.

The verdict, in the words of Section 1, unchanged:

> The Poincaré row is closed to one act of existence. Existence, its root proved at theorem grade with no hypothesis on its constructed domain and as the law of every root that grounds itself (Appendix R), universally presupposed and undeniable in deed, carries the form of the closure on every frame and holds in the round world and the fake-sphere world alike. Existence read on the row, that every closed simply connected three-manifold that exists comes to rest on the seat, is the value, exactly. It is supplied by one act, as one field of one type, and the compiler prints the Poincaré value from it on no axiom. Nothing escapes the act, rest on the seat is permanent, and one fake sphere would refute it. The Perelman witness, in the shape the kernel types, strict descent off the seat, supplies the act and is a strict subtype of the bridge; the historical functional inhabits that shape on the surgical route where its descent is strict, as Section 10 states. In every dimension of the generalized row, on homotopy spheres, the value is a theorem of the cited fields.

The root on which this paper stands is proved here, in this paper, so that no reader needs another document to check it. A root grounds itself when deeds occur and every deed instances it (\thm{SelfGrounding}); the root kernel's own comments call a deed an act. The constructed domain has one existent, whose energy of actuation is one, so the Root Axiom there reads $\forall x,\ 0<\Delta E_0(x)$ (\thm{RA₀}). On that domain the Root Axiom is a theorem with no axiom and no hypothesis (\thm{root_on_the_constructed_domain}). For every root that grounds itself, the root holds, every deed re-enacts it, and no outside proof adds anything to it, one closed theorem on no axiom (\thm{the_floor_is_universal}). Every denial of the root is a deed that instances it (\thm{denial_reenacts_root}); the root is held by its deed (\thm{seated_undeniable}); it is self-verifying (\thm{denial_instantiates}); and no level stands above it (\thm{no_level_above}). The master seal binds the root's universal law and the constructed root with the Return, the scalar line as the fixed set of conjugation on the chart, and the twelve gates counted three ways, as the Hurwitz units up to sign, as the norm-two shell up to sign and as the even permutations of four points with class equation 1, 3, 4, 4, in one theorem on no axiom (\thm{the_master_seal}); it states the three counts side by side and no map between them. The body of this paper reads the root on its row; this appendix is the root itself. The row kernel repeats the constructed root verbatim, proves it is the root on one existent with actuation one, and locks it to the row (\thm{RA₀_is_the_constructed_root}, \thm{the_lock_on_the_root}). ## Author's Provenance and Method Disclosure {-}

This paper was developed under Trisduction, a verification and organizing discipline that adds the cited results no warrant; the method and its executable batteries are stated in full in the master reference (Islam 2026j).

*Author and method.* Mohammad F. Islam, PhD, independent researcher, sole author and sole authority for every claim and every error. The reduction was directed by the author and drafted with Claude (Anthropic) as scribe: the model wrote and compiled the kernel, checked citations and argued against the author's positions on instruction; it holds no authorship. One claim was held at the model's insistence: the kernel reads the Perelman functional by its shape and does not formalize it, and the paper says so wherever the witness is named: the Perelman witness, in the shape the kernel types, strict descent off the seat, supplies the act and is a strict subtype of the bridge, and the historical functional inhabits that shape on the surgical route where its descent is strict, as Section 10 states.

*The register.* In the programme's vocabulary: the form, the act's identity with the value, the universal closure, the bridge and the typing of the witness stand at [{\symfont ⟀}\,T] on no axiom, with the master's normal form, binding, row split, walls, closure of the question and ten steps, one hundred ninety-seven theorems in all; the Mathlib binding at the grade of Lean's standard axioms; the triaxial reading at [{\symfont ⟀}\,S]; the value in every dimension of the generalized row at the grade of the cited theorems; $\Delta M=0$ on the cited topology. The act is the row's least-erasure posit read as existence at rest; the seat is the round state. The Grand Witness of the author's Offering Bit is placed by theorem: an offering of the act, one supply among those the act admits. The freedom cut, prime-as-freedom and the triaxial lock are applied, with the lessons of the six earlier closures combined.

*Audit.* This edition has not yet been prosecuted by the external substrates, and no audit result is claimed. The development provenance is the author's Zenodo trail, the Unabridged Trisduction Codex and the Lean Codex at github.com/1000sapients/Trisduction.

::: {.box title="Nature of Root Axiom"}
**The root is triaxial, and that is the force of the cut.** A characterless existence has no triaxiality: one scalar, no fold, no seat, nothing to lock. The root, to exist is to actuate, carries three axes, and they are not chosen: for every actuation, the direction of its deed gives the fold, the registration and the seat, and the registration is the only height-keeping map onto the seat. The deed is the direction: a denial spends the deed and instances the root. The registration is the fold's record: it keeps the height and forgets the side, and the two even axes, symmetry and record, are closed under combination and blind. The seat is the fixed set, where the two blind spots cancel, and the third axis is the one orientation the even pair cannot supply. Two axes never determine the point; three do. The seven rows are one cut because each is an involution with a seat and one missing orientation. The root makes the third axis native as a place: the seat, and the one orientation still open there; the axes themselves force no orientation. Which way that orientation points is the value, and it is the root read on that seat. The root alone still forces no value.

*Key to the statement, in the words of its receipts.* The three axes are the two even axes, the fold's symmetry and the record, and the orientation, the side of a point of the actuation, which the even pair cannot supply; the fold, the registration and the seat are what the direction of the deed gives, and the seat is where the side is read. The point two axes never determine is a point off the seat: there the even pair does not tell a point from its reversal, and the record with the side locks the point; on the seat both blind spots cancel and nothing is open there to determine. A point of the actuation is a side and a height, and every point instances the root: the points are the actuation's deeds, and the deed is the direction in the sense that its side is its direction, reversed by the fold. The characterless case, one scalar, is a degenerate direction: the fold is the identity and the seat is total, which is to say no fold and no seat to lock. The axes are not chosen in the sense that, given the direction, the fold, the registration and the seat are forced, the registration the only height-keeping map onto the seat; the root's own actuation takes the integer direction, under which it is not characterless, while a degenerate direction over the same deeds would be (\thm{every_root_actuates}, \thm{degenerate_is_characterless}). The root read on that seat is the root read on the row at the seat point where a point and its reversal land with one record; that the orientation between them is the row's value bit is the paper's structural reading, a second statement beside its kernel's theorems, and no kernel states a map between the two. Each row carries the actuation's seat as the root of an actuation over its own deeds; a row's own seat, where its kernel states one, is a further statement of that kernel, named in the last row of the table, and on P versus NP it is vacant. A deed of the root is an inhabitant of its type of acts, which the root kernel's own comments call an act; in the root's actuation those deeds sit at the zero side, on the seat, and the points off the seat are the sides the direction supplies; the act, unqualified, is existence read on the row, the field the closing theorem consumes.

*Receipts, each cited for what its statement says; R.1, R.2 and R.3 are the three kernels of Appendix R, every theorem of each on no axiom; the three theorems of the Master Codex are cited, not carried.*

|---|---|
| the root carries three axes, not chosen: every self-grounding root is the root of an actuation over its own deeds, and for every actuation, given the direction of its deed, the fold, the registration and the seat are forced, the registration the only height-keeping map onto the seat | \thm{every_root_actuates}, \thm{the_root_carries_three_axes}, \thm{actuation_is_triaxial}, \thm{registration_is_forced} (Appendix R.2) |
| the registration is the only height-keeping map onto the seat | \thm{registration_is_forced} (R.2) |
| the axes force no orientation | \thm{axes_force_no_orientation} (R.2) |
| the deed is the direction: a deed of the actuation is a point, a side and a height, its side its direction, reversed by the fold; a reversed point is still a deed and instances the root; a denial spends the deed and instances the root | \thm{fold}, \thm{denial_instances_root} (R.2); \thm{denial_reenacts_root}, \thm{denial_instantiates} (R.1) |
| the registration is the fold's record: it keeps the height, forgets the side and reads a point and its reversal alike; the fold is an involution; the even pair is closed under combination | \thm{registration_is_the_folds_record}, \thm{reg_forgets_side}, \thm{reg_keeps_height}, \thm{fold_involutive}, \thm{fold_of_reg} (R.2) |
| the two even axes are blind: no reading of the record returns the orientation; symmetry keyless, the line property keyed | \thm{readings_of_the_record_are_blind}, \thm{tongue_freedom} (R.2); `symmetry_is_keyless`, `line_property_is_keyed`, `exactly_one_stands` (Islam 2026l, Master Codex v5.1.0, commit 5e55fca, cited for their statements, not carried; that Codex declares its root as an axiom, this paper's kernels declare none) |
| the seat is the fixed set, where both blind spots cancel; off the seat both stand | \thm{seat_iff_zero_side}, \thm{seat_cancels_both}, \thm{off_seat_both}, \thm{the_only_special_cut} (R.2) |
| the registration lands on the seat; off the seat a point and its reversal land on one seat point with one record, and the orientation is what stays open there; every reading of the record reads both alike | \thm{reg_lands_on_seat}, \thm{open_at_the_seat}, \thm{record_leaves_the_orientation}, \thm{readings_of_the_record_are_blind} (R.2) |
| the characterless case, no fold and no seat to lock: over a degenerate direction, one side only, the fold is the identity and every point is on the seat; the root's actuation is not degenerate | \thm{degenerate_is_characterless}, \thm{intDir_not_degenerate}, \thm{every_root_actuates} (R.2) |
| the bridge atom is an actuation, and not characterless | \thm{the_bridge_atom}, \thm{the_atom_is_not_characterless}, \thm{the_triaxial_seal} (R.2) |
| the scalar ground in the algebra, a third reading beside the actuation's: the Return, the line the conjugation fixes and the twelve gates, bound with the root | \thm{the_return}, \thm{the_line_is_the_fixed_set}, \thm{twenty_four_units_twelve_gates}, \thm{the_class_equation}, \thm{the_master_seal} (R.1) |
| the root at theorem grade on the Codex's ledger rule, the grade read from the evidence the root is entered with, a warrant reading theorem and a posit premise, and not elected, and RA–Tongue co-location inheriting it | \thm{a_warranted_root_is_theorem_grade}, \thm{the_ledger_reads_the_warrant}, \thm{the_grade_is_not_elected}, \thm{ra_is_theorem_grade}, \thm{coloc_grade_is_the_roots_grade}, \thm{the_ledger_seal} (R.3) |
| two axes never determine the point; three do: off the seat the even pair does not tell a point from its reversal, and the record with the side, which carries the orientation, locks the point; on the seat both blind spots cancel and nothing is open to determine | \thm{even_pair_leaves_two}, \thm{orientation_locks_the_act}, \thm{seat_cancels_both} (R.2); in its own coordinates, for independent axes in $\mathbb{F}_2^3$, \thm{two_axes_leave_two}, \thm{three_axes_lock_one} (this paper's kernel), a second statement |
| the root alone forces no value; the root read on the row is the value | \thm{undeniable_root_forces_no_value}; \thm{root_on_row_is_the_value}, \thm{act_is_the_value} (this paper's kernel) |
| the seven rows are one cut: each row's root is an actuation, so each carries an involution, the fold, with a seat and one missing orientation | \thm{every_root_actuates}, \thm{the_root_carries_three_axes} (R.2), stated for every self-grounding root and read at the constructed root \thm{ra₀} of this paper's kernel, whose structure repeats R.2's \thm{SelfGrounding} field for field; the row's own seat is the round point, fixed under the step (\thm{seat_is_fixed}), a statement of this kernel beside the actuation's seat; the kernel states no involution of its own beyond the actuation's fold; the row ledger of Islam (2026f, Book III), cited |
:::

/-
  PC_Normal_Form_Paper_v1_1_0_CC.lean · the kernel of "The Poincaré Conjecture: Unconditional Proof of the Form by

  Part A (namespace PC.NORMALFORM) is the normal form of the row, generic over a row and read on the Poincaré row as

  the keyed bit; the record deciding nothing; every route passing the bit; the keyless wall; no uniform answer.
  Part B (namespace PC.HOST) is the host model and its binding, proved: the record of a frame starts every manifold

  Part C (namespace PC.ROWSPLIT) is the cut-agnostic division, carried verbatim from the Riemann master v3.0.39_CC,
  and Part C′ (namespace PC.REALPART) reads it by dimension: the real part, every dimension but three, sealed by the

  Part D (namespace PC.WALLS): the tree count and the degree record decided by the kernel, the record that reads every

  Part E (namespace PC.RESIDUE): the one implication that remains, printed as a hypothesis and assumed nowhere; its

  THE TWO-LAYER CLOSURE. Everything except the bit is proved with no posit: the floor on no axiom at all, and every
  other theorem of this file on no axiom at all; and the bit, the row's reading, is all that remains, at premise grade.
  The root alone forces no value; the root read on the row is the value. No declaration of this file has the type of
